pdfplumber\n\npdf_path = Path('/root/employees_backup.pdf')\nxlsx_path = Path('/root/employees_current.xlsx')\n\nid_re = re.compile(r'\"'^EMP'\"\\\\d{5}\"'$'\"')\n\ncolumns = ['ID','First','Last','Dept','Position','Salary','Years','Location','Score']\n\nrows = []\nwith pdfplumber.open(pdf_path) as pdf:\n    for page in pdf.pages:\n        tables = page.extract_tables() or []\n        for table in tables:\n            if not table:\n                continue\n            # Determine header\n            header = None\n            first_row = [c.strip() if isinstance(c, str) else c for c in table[0]]\n            if first_row and first_row[0] == 'ID':\n                header = first_row\n                data_rows = table[1:]\n            else:\n                header = columns\n                data_rows = table\n            for row in data_rows:\n                if not row:\n                    continue\n                # Normalize length\n                # Pad or
